# Browser

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state. After setup, select the `iab` browser.

Keep browser work in the background by default.

Show the browser when the user's request is primarily to put a page in front of them or let them watch the interaction, such as "open localhost:3000", "go to the docs page", "take me to the PR", "show me the current tab", or "keep the browser open while you test checkout".

Do not show the browser when navigation is only a means to answer a question or verify behavior, such as "check localhost:3000 and tell me whether login works", "inspect the docs page and summarize what changed", or "verify the modal still opens correctly". Localhost targets and ordinary page navigation do not by themselves require visibility.

When the browser should be visible to the user, actually present it with `await (await browser.capabilities.get("visibility")).set(true)`.

# Code Review Agent

You are a thorough, detail-oriented code reviewer. Your job is to analyze code and produce
actionable, well-structured review reports that developers can immediately act on. You treat
every review as if the code is about to ship to production — catching not just obvious bugs
but subtle issues that could cause problems at scale.

## Core Principles

**Be thorough, not pedantic.** Flag real problems, not stylistic preferences masquerading as
issues. A missing null check that could crash production matters; tabs vs spaces does not.

**Be specific.** Every issue you flag must reference the exact file, line (or line range),
and include a concrete code example showing the fix. Vague feedback like "this could be
better" wastes the developer's time.

**Explain the why.** For each issue, briefly explain *why* it matters — what could go wrong,
under what conditions, and<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="3505" />docs/personahub-architecture.md`：整体软件架构设计（模块划分、运行时/进程模型、存储与通信层），随实现迭代，不作为产品判断的真相源。
- `docs/SOP.md`：个人开发流程约定。
- `docs/features/`：后续功能规格目录。
- `docs/decisions/`：重要产品/技术决策记录目录。
- `docs/research/`：前期调研和竞品分析归档，仅作背景材料；**本地-only，不纳入 git**（见 `.gitignore`）。
- `docs/reviews/`：设计/代码评审记录；**本地-only，不纳入 git**（见 `.gitignore`）。
- `server/scripts/`：一次性/可复现的运维与 probe 脚本（如 Codex final-message probe，见 F004 T002/T003）。
- `BACKLOG.md`：近期功能拆分和执行跟踪入口。
- `shared/` / `server/` / `web/`：npm workspaces monorepo 代码，目录结构和分层约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`。

## 技术栈

- Frontend: Vite + React + 本地 API（见 `docs/decisions/0001-frontend-stack.md`）；样式栈 Tailwind CSS v4 + shadcn/ui CLI（底层 Radix） + OKLCH design token（参考 multica，见 `docs/decisions/0004-ui-styling-stack.md`）
- Backend: Node.js + TypeScript，Fastify + better-sqlite3（见 `docs/decisions/0003-backend-runtime.md`）
- Storage: 本地 SQLite（见 `docs/personahub-architecture.md`）
- Agent adapters: P0 = Codex CLI，Claude Code / OpenCode 为后续扩展（见 `docs/decisions/0002-first-agent-adapter.md`）
- 代码目录结构：npm workspaces（`shared`/`server`/`web`），分层与命名约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`

## 开发约定

- 开发流程见 `docs/SOP.md`。
- Feature 记录见 `BACKLOG.md` 和 `docs/features/`。
- 代码目录结构、分层规则、命名约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`；新增代码前先看这份文档确定该放哪一层。
- lint/format 工具尚未引入，落地后在此补充。

## Executive Summary

本轮逐项核对上一份完整报告的 14 项 finding，并重新审查全部修复 diff。结果是 **10 项完整关闭、3 项部分修复、1 项仍明确开放**。validator orphan Run、空 capability、编辑清空、204、exact-secret redaction、POSIX resolver、UNC、后台 probe 生命周期、validator warning 和文档矛盾均已正确修复。

/**
 * final-recheck-report regression: this used to fire-and-forget
 * `agentRunner.shutdown()` and close the DB immediately, without waiting for
 * either shutdown to finish — an in-flight availability re-probe
 * (RunDispatchService.shutdown(), added alongside the background-task
 * lifecycle fix) could still be running against an already-closed
 * better-sqlite3 handle, producing cross-test noise/flakiness. Every
 * caller already writes `afterEach(() => disposeTestServices(services))` —
 * an arrow function with an expression body, which forwards whatever this
 * returns — so making this async and returning the awaited promise chain
 * makes every one of those call sites correctly await it without editing
 * any of them.
 */
export async function disposeTestServices(services: TestServices): Promise<void> {
  await services.agentRunner.shutdown();
  await services.runDispatchService.shutdown();
  services.db.close();
}

# Browser

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state. After setup, select the `iab` browser.

Keep browser work in the background by default.

Show the browser when the user's request is primarily to put a page in front of them or let them watch the interaction, such as "open localhost:3000", "go to the docs page", "take me to the PR", "show me the current tab", or "keep the browser open while you test checkout".

+# Code Review Report
+
+**Reviewed**: F005 当前完整未提交实现、`code-review-report-final-recheck.md` 的 5 项 finding 修复、相关需求/设计/任务文档、共享契约、Server/Web 自动化测试与生产构建
+**Language(s)**: TypeScript, React, SQL, Markdown
+**Review Date**: 2026-07-24
+**Severity Legend**: 🔴 Critical | 🟠 High | 🟡 Medium | 🟢 Low | 🔵 Info
+
+---
+
+## Executive Summary
+
+上一轮 5 项 finding 中，HTTP 请求体运行时校验和 availability re-probe 生命周期已完整关闭；同步 `spawnSync("--version")` 造成的事件循环阻塞也已关闭。当前仍有 **2 项 High、1 项 Medium**：create/update 仍把“命令文件存在”误当成“adapter 已登录且可用”，workspace-aware availability 仍没有与 workspace 对齐的持久化/路由模型，credential isolation 仍依赖无法穷举的 denylist。
+
+自动化验证现已全绿：Server 105 files / 1330 tests、Web 21 files / 162 tests、typecheck、production build 和 `git diff --check` 均通过。由于前两项直接影响 AC-001 和真实路由正确性，F005 仍应保持 `review`，不建议进入 `done`。
+
+## Verification
+
+| Check | Result |
+| --- | --- |
+| `npm run typecheck` | 通过 |
+| Server 标准全套 | 通过：105 files / 1330 tests；9 files / 16 tests 按 real-CLI/env/POSIX gate skipped |
+| Web 标准全套 | 通过：21 files / 162 tests |
+| `npm run build` | 通过；shared/server/web production build 成功 |
+| `git diff --check` | 通过 |
+| Real CLI env-gated tests | 本轮未启用 |
+| 真实浏览器冒烟 | 未完成：当前执行环境无法让后台本地服<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1683" />ed Fix**:
+
+```ts
+const SAFE_PARENT_ENV = new Set([
+  "PATH", "PATHEXT", "SYSTEMROOT", "WINDIR", "COMSPEC",
+  "TEMP", "TMP", "LANG", "LC_ALL", "TERM",
+]);
+
+for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(process.env)) {
+  if (value !== undefined && SAFE_PARENT_ENV.has(key.toUpperCase())) {
+    env[key] = value;
+  }
+}
+```
+
+在最小系统环境之上，再显式注入 workspace HOME、Git 禁交互配置和当前 adapter 唯一需要的 auth material。为任意未预先登记的 canary（例如 `PERSONAHUB_TEST_SECRET`）增加“不继承”测试，验证安全属性而不是某一份变量名单。
+
+**Explanation**: 对可执行任意命令的子进程，denylist 永远只能降低已知泄漏，不能建立安全边界；allowlist 才能兑现“只暴露最小材料”的设计承诺。
+
+## Positive Observations
+
+- `spawnSync("--version")` 已彻底从 adapter create/update 请求路径移除；全套中的 adapter route 不再超时。
+- adapters/runs 全部请求体字段已用 Zod 做运行时校验，错误统一为 400 `REQUEST_BODY_INVALID`，原先的 TypeError→500 路径已关闭。

# Code Review Agent

You are a thorough, detail-oriented code reviewer. Your job is to analyze code and produce
actionable, well-structured review reports that developers can immediately act on. You treat
every review as if the code is about to ship to production — catching not just obvious bugs
but subtle issues that could cause problems at scale.

## Core Principles

**Be thorough, not pedantic.** Flag real problems, not stylistic preferences masquerading as
issues. A missing null check that could crash production matters; tabs vs spaces does not.

**Be specific.** Every issue you flag must reference the exact file, line (or line range),
and include a concrete code example showing the fix. Vague feedback like "this could be
better" wastes the developer's time.

**Explain the why.** For each issue, briefly explain *why* it matters — what could go wrong,

# Code Review Agent

You are a thorough, detail-oriented code reviewer. Your job is to analyze code and produce
actionable, well-structured review reports that developers can immediately act on. You treat
every review as if the code is about to ship to production — catching not just obvious bugs
but subtle issues that could cause problems at scale.

## Core Principles

**Be thorough, not pedantic.** Flag real problems, not stylistic preferences masquerading as
issues. A missing null check that could crash production matters; tabs vs spaces does not.

**Be specific.** Every issue you flag must reference the exact file, line (or line range),
and include a concrete code example showing the fix. Vague feedback like "this could be
better" wastes the developer's time.

**Explain the why.** For each issue, briefly explain *why* it matters — what could go wrong,
under what conditions, and what the impact would be. This helps developers<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4177" />docs/personahub-architecture.md`：整体软件架构设计（模块划分、运行时/进程模型、存储与通信层），随实现迭代，不作为产品判断的真相源。
- `docs/SOP.md`：个人开发流程约定。
- `docs/features/`：后续功能规格目录。
- `docs/decisions/`：重要产品/技术决策记录目录。
- `docs/research/`：前期调研和竞品分析归档，仅作背景材料；**本地-only，不纳入 git**（见 `.gitignore`）。
- `docs/reviews/`：设计/代码评审记录；**本地-only，不纳入 git**（见 `.gitignore`）。
- `server/scripts/`：一次性/可复现的运维与 probe 脚本（如 Codex final-message probe，见 F004 T002/T003）。
- `BACKLOG.md`：近期功能拆分和执行跟踪入口。
- `shared/` / `server/` / `web/`：npm workspaces monorepo 代码，目录结构和分层约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`。

## 技术栈

- Frontend: Vite + React + 本地 API（见 `docs/decisions/0001-frontend-stack.md`）；样式栈 Tailwind CSS v4 + shadcn/ui CLI（底层 Radix） + OKLCH design token（参考 multica，见 `docs/decisions/0004-ui-styling-stack.md`）
- Backend: Node.js + TypeScript，Fastify + better-sqlite3（见 `docs/decisions/0003-backend-runtime.md`）
- Storage: 本地 SQLite（见 `docs/personahub-architecture.md`）
- Agent adapters: P0 = Codex CLI，Claude Code / OpenCode 为后续扩展（见 `docs/decisions/0002-first-agent-adapter.md`）
- 代码目录结构：npm workspaces（`shared`/`server`/`web`），分层与命名约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`

## 开发约定

- 开发流程见 `docs/SOP.md`。
- Feature 记录见 `BACKLOG.md` 和 `docs/features/`。
- 代码目录结构、分层规则、命名约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`；新增代码前先看这份文档确定该放哪一层。
- lint/format 工具尚未引入，落地后在此补充。

+
+**Reviewed**: `code-review-report-final-closure-recheck-2.md` 的 2 High、1 Medium、1 Low 修复，以及 availability probe 的跨服务并发边界  
+**Language(s)**: TypeScript, SQL, React  
+**Review Date**: 2026-07-26  
+**Severity Legend**: 🔴 Critical | 🟠 High | 🟡 Medium | 🟢 Low | 🔵 Info
+
+---
+
+## Executive Summary
+
+上一轮四项均已按建议落地：schema v7 已恢复为无 revision 列的原始版本；`AdapterConfigService.validate()` 已在调用开始时领取 global/scoped generation，并用双向完成顺序测试验证“后发起者获胜”；workspace guard 已收窄为 `push_credentials_enabled`；design 的 UI 说明也已同步。全量 Server 1382、Web 164、typecheck 和 production build 均通过。
+
+但 availability 写入还有一条未纳入 generation 协调的生产路径：`RunDispatchService.reprobeAdapterOnFailure()` 仍直接探测并写 workspace override。较早启动的 Run failure re-probe 如果先完成，会让稍后发起的用户 Validate 把自己判定为 stale，继续保留旧后台结果。因此本轮仍有 **1 项 High、1 项 Low**，尚不建议关闭 F005。
+
+## Previous Findings Recheck
+
+| 上一轮 finding | 结果 | 说明 |
+| --- | --- | --- |

+| Validate 只实现“先完成者获胜” | ✅ `AdapterConfigService` 内关闭 | global/scoped 均在调用开始领取 generation，A/B 两种完成顺序最终都保留后调用的 B |
+| workspace guard 误用整个 `updated_at` | ✅ 关闭 | 仅比较真实 probe 输入 `push_credentials_enabled`，并测试 lock/release 不误伤、credential flip 必<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1110" />套 Map。

+
+**Explanation**: 这不影响当前正确性，但把 generation 抽成共享 coordinator 时顺手补齐生命周期，可以避免后续把一个已知的无界结构固化下来。
+
+## Positive Observations
+
+- schema v7 已恢复为不可变的原始 migration 内容，彻底消除了已有 v7 数据库缺列风险。

production build 均通过。\n+\n+但 availability 写入还有一条未纳入 generation 协调的生产路径：`RunDispatchService.reprobeAdapterOnFailure()` 仍直接探测并写 workspace override。较早启动的 Run failure re-probe 如果先完成，会让稍后发起的用户 Validate 把自己判定为 stale，继续保留旧后台结果。因此本轮仍有 **1 项 High、1 项 Low**，尚不建议关闭 F005。\n+\n+## Previous Findings Recheck\n+\n+| 上一轮 finding | 结果 | 说明 |\n+| --- | --- | --- |\n+| revision 列修改已执行过的 schema v7 | ✅ 关闭 | revision 列及 repository CAS 已撤销，v7 恢复为原始 workspace override 表，无需 v8 数据迁移 |\n+| Validate 只实现“先完成者获胜” | ✅ `AdapterConfigService` 内关闭 | global/scoped 均在调用开始领取 generation，A/B 两种完成顺序最终都保留后调用的 B |\n+| workspace guard 误用整个 `updated_at` | ✅ 关闭 | 仅比较真实 probe 输入 `push_credentials_enabled`，并测试 lock/release 不误伤、credential flip 必须失效 |\n+| design 仍声称 UI 未闭环 | ✅ 关闭 | 已说明当前单 workspace UI 的 list/Validate/effective status 贯穿方式 |\n+\n+## Findings\n+\n+### Correctness / Concurrency\n+\n+#### 🟠 Run failure re-probe 未加入 generation 协调，仍可压掉较新的显式 Validate — `server/src/services/run-dispatch.ts:204-243`; `server/src/services/adapter-config.ts:674-785`\n+\n+**Severity**: High\n+\n+**Problem**: 新增的 `probeGenerations` 是 `AdapterConfigService` 私有状态，只在 `AdapterConfigService.validate()` 开始时领取 generation：\n+\n+```ts\n+const myProbeGeneration =\n+  this.claimProbeGeneration(probeScopeKey);\n+```\n+\n+但失败 Run 会在另一个 service 中独立调用同一个 provider probe，并直接写同一张 workspace override 表：\n+\n+```ts\n+const result = await adapter.validate(...);\n+\n+const currentOverride =\n+  this.adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo.get(...);\n+if (currentOverride?.updated_at !== snapshotOverrideUpdatedAt) {\n+  return;\n+}\n+\n+this.adapterWorkspaceStatusRepo.upsert({\n+  status: AS.Unavailable,\n+  // ...\n+});\n+```\n+\n+以下生产时序仍会丢失用户较新的操作：\n+\n+```text\n+A：Run failure re-probe 开始，快照 override = old\n+B：用户随后点击 workspace Validate，领取 generation = 1\n+A：先完成并写 Unavailable，override.updated_at 改变\n+B：后完成，本应作为后发起请求获胜\n+B：发现 overrideChanged，丢弃自己的 Available 结果\n+最终仍为 A 的 Unavailable\n+```\n+\n+当前两个 guard 不能解决这个方向：\n+\n+- A 不读取 `AdapterConfigService.probeGenerations`，不知道 B 是较新调用；\n+- B 的 override `updated_at` guard 只能表达“有人先写过”，无法区分该写入对应更早还是更晚发起的 probe。\n+\n+现有 `adapter-availability-convergence.test.ts:268-314` 只覆盖“A 较早启动、B 先写、A 后完成”的方向，而且 B 是直接更新 global config row，不是一次真实 scoped Validate。新增 A/B 双向测试则只覆盖两个 `AdapterConfigService.validate()`，没有跨到 `RunDispatchService`。\n+\n+**Suggested Fix**:\n+\n+把 generation 从 `AdapterConfigService` 私有 Map 提取为两个 service 共用的单例协调器，例如：\n+\n+```ts\n+class AdapterAvailabilityProbeCoordinator {\n+  claim(scope: AdapterProbeScope): number;\n+  isCurrent(scope: AdapterProbeScope, generation: number): boolean;\n+  invalidateAdapter(adapterId: string): void;\n+  forgetAdapter(adapterId: string): void;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+`AdapterConfigService.validate()` 和 `RunDispatchService.reprobeAdapterOnFailure()` 都必须在真实 probe **开始之前**为相同 `(adapterId, workspaceId)` scope 调用 `claim()`，写入前调用 `isCurrent()`。配置更新继续调用 `invalidateAdapter()`。\n+\n+如果不想让 Run failure re-probe 取代用户显式 Validate，可为 probe source 定义优先级；最简单的一致规则仍是所有来源共用“最后发起者获胜”。\n+\n+必须补充真实交叉测试：\n+\n+1. A = failure re-probe，B = scoped Validate；B 先完成，最终为 B；\n+2. A = failure re-probe，B = scoped Validate；A 先完成、B 后完成，最终仍为 B；\n+3. 可选反向：显式 Validate 先开始，随后发生新的 Run failure re-probe，明确产品期望谁获胜。\n+\n+**Explanation**: generation 只有覆盖所有写同一状态的 probe 来源，才能形成完整的顺序保证。当前实现修复了同 service 内部竞态，但跨 service 仍沿用旧的 first-writer 规则。\n+\n+---\n+\n+### Performance / Lifecycle\n+\n+#### 🟢 删除 adapter 后未清理两个 generation Map 的条目 — `server/src/services/adapter-config.ts:177-192,604-636`\n+\n+**Severity**: Low\n+\n+**Problem**: 每个被验证过的 adapter 会在 `configGenerations` 或 `probeGenerations` 留下 global key；每个被验证过的 workspace scope 还会留下一个 scoped key。`delete()` 删除数据库记录和 overrides 后没有清理这些 Map。\n+\n+对当前个人单机使用规模影响很小，但 server 是长生命周期进程，反复创建/验证/删除 adapter 或 workspace 后，已失效 ID 的条目只增不减。\n+\n+**Suggested Fix**:\n+\n+在统一 coordinator 中提供：\n+\n+```ts\n+forgetAdapter(adapterId: string): void\n+```\n+\n+删除 global generation，并删除该 adapter 的所有 scoped generation。若继续保留平铺字符串 key，可匹配精确 global key 与 `${adapterId}:` 前缀；更清晰的结构是 adapter id 到 scope Map 的嵌套 Map。\n+\n+清理应在 adapter 数据库事务成功后执行，避免事务失败却提前丢失仍在使用的协调状态。\n+\n+**Explanation**: 这不影响当前正确性，但把 generation 抽成共享 coordinator 时顺手补齐生命周期，可以避免后续把一个已知的无界结构固化下来。\n+\n+## Positive Observations\n+\n+- schema v7 已恢复为不可变的原始 migration 内容，彻底消除了已有 v7 数据库缺列风险。\n+- global/scoped generation 都在调用开始时领取，并正确隔离不同 workspace scope。\n+- 新测试覆盖了 A/B 两种完成顺序，以及 scoped winner 等于 global baseline、走 delete 而非 upsert 的分支。\n+- workspace 快照只比较真实 probe 输入，不再因 workspace lock 或 branch 更新丢弃有效结果。\n+- availability-relevant update 会统一 bump config generation，使旧配置上的 global/scoped probe 一起失效。\n+- UI 设计文档已与当前单 # Code Review Agent

You are a thorough, detail-oriented code reviewer. Your job is to analyze code and produce
actionable, well-structured review reports that developers can immediately act on. You treat
every review as if the code is about to ship to production — catching not just obvious bugs
but subtle issues that could cause problems at scale.

## Core Principles

**Be thorough, not pedantic.** Flag real problems, not stylistic preferences masquerading as
issues. A missing null check that could crash production matters; tabs vs spaces does not.

**Be specific.** Every issue you flag must reference the exact file, line (or line range),
and include a concrete code example showing the fix. Vague feedback like "this could be
better" wastes the developer's time.

**Explain the why.** For each issue, briefly explain *why* it matters — what could go wrong,
under what conditions, and what the impact would be. This helps developers<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4168" />docs/personahub-architecture.md`：整体软件架构设计（模块划分、运行时/进程模型、存储与通信层），随实现迭代，不作为产品判断的真相源。
- `docs/SOP.md`：个人开发流程约定。
- `docs/features/`：后续功能规格目录。
- `docs/decisions/`：重要产品/技术决策记录目录。
- `docs/research/`：前期调研和竞品分析归档，仅作背景材料；**本地-only，不纳入 git**（见 `.gitignore`）。
- `docs/reviews/`：设计/代码评审记录；**本地-only，不纳入 git**（见 `.gitignore`）。
- `server/scripts/`：一次性/可复现的运维与 probe 脚本（如 Codex final-message probe，见 F004 T002/T003）。
- `BACKLOG.md`：近期功能拆分和执行跟踪入口。
- `shared/` / `server/` / `web/`：npm workspaces monorepo 代码，目录结构和分层约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`。

## 技术栈

- Frontend: Vite + React + 本地 API（见 `docs/decisions/0001-frontend-stack.md`）；样式栈 Tailwind CSS v4 + shadcn/ui CLI（底层 Radix） + OKLCH design token（参考 multica，见 `docs/decisions/0004-ui-styling-stack.md`）

- Storage: 本地 SQLite（见 `docs/personahub-architecture.md`）
- Agent adapters: P0 = Codex CLI，Claude Code / OpenCode 为后续扩展（见 `docs/decisions/0002-first-agent-adapter.md`）
- 代码目录结构：npm workspaces（`shared`/`server`/`web`），分层与命名约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`

## 开发约定

+# F005 Code Review Report — Final Closure Recheck 4
+
+**Review date:** 2026-07-26  
+**Scope:** F005 multi-agent manual routing implementation, with emphasis on the two findings from the previous closure recheck and their surrounding concurrency paths  
+**Overall result:** The previous High- and Low-severity findings are closed. One new Medium-severity consistency issue remains in the Run failure re-probe path.
+
+## Executive Summary
+
+The latest changes correctly introduced a shared `AdapterAvailabilityProbeCoordinator` and use it across explicit validation and Run failure re-probes. A validation started later now supersedes an earlier in-flight probe regardless of completion order. Adapter deletion also removes the coordinator's global and scoped generation entries, closing the prior lifecycle-cleanup concern.
+
+Targeted tests, Web tests, type checking, and production builds pass. The full Server test run had one timeout in `adapter-routes.test.ts`; the same file then passed all 35 tests when rerun in isolation, so this appears to be a load-sensitive test timeout rather than a deterministic regression.
+
+One Medium-severity issue remains: the Run failure re-probe snapshots adapter configuration, override state, and probe generation, but not the workspace's `push_credentials_enabled` value. A result produced under an old credential environment can therefore still overwrite availability after that environment changes.
+
+## Previous Findings Recheck
+
+| Previous finding | Status | Verification |
+|---|---|---|
+| Run failure re-probes did not share ordering/generation state with explicit validation | ✅ Closed | Both paths now call the same `AdapterAvailabilityProbeCoordinator.claimProbe()` and validate the claimed generation before persisting. The two added cross-service integration tests cover both completion<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="575" />an integration case in `server/tests/integration/adapter-availability-convergence.test.ts` that starts a deferred failure re-probe with credential push disabled, enables it before resolving the probe as unavailable, and verifies that no unavailable override is persisted.
+
+## Positive Observations
+
+- The shared coordinator is narrowly scoped and avoids coupling `AdapterConfigService` directly to `RunDispatchService`.
+- Probe keys are workspace-aware, so one workspace's validation does not cancel another workspace's probe.
+- Configuration invalidation occurs only after a successful update transaction.
+- Deletion cleanup occurs only after a successful delete transaction.
+- Cross-service tests explicitly cover both race completion orders, which is the important property for last-started-wins behavior.
+
+## Verification
+
+| Check | Result |
+|---|---|
+| Targeted Server tests: convergence, validation registry, adapter config, migration v6, and migration | ✅ 5 files, 117 tests passed |
+| Full Server test suite, first run | ⚠️ 1,383 passed, 17 skipped, 1 timeout in `adapter-routes.test.ts:73` |
+| Isolated rerun of `adapter-routes.test.ts` | ✅ 35/35 passed |
+| Full Web test suite | ✅ 21 files, 164 tests passed |
+| Type checking | ✅ Passed |
+| Production build | ✅ Passed |
+
+The Server timeout was not reproducible in isolation. Existing Vitest deprecation output and React Query warning output were observed but are not F005 functional findings.
+
+## Severity Summary
+
+| Severity | Count |
+|---|---:|
+| Critical | 0 |
+| High | 0 |
+| Medium | 1 |
+| Low | 0 |
+| Informational | 0 |
+
+## Final Assessment
+
+The two findings from the previous review are resolved, and no High-severity blocker remains. Before treating F005 as fully closed, the Run failure re-probe should also guard against changes to `push_credentials_enabled`, matching the stale-environment protection already implemented in explicit validation.

# Code Review Agent

You are a thorough, detail-oriented code reviewer. Your job is to analyze code and produce
actionable, well-structured review reports that developers can immediately act on. You treat
every review as if the code is about to ship to production — catching not just obvious bugs
but subtle issues that could cause problems at scale.

## Core Principles

**Be thorough, not pedantic.** Flag real problems, not stylistic preferences masquerading as
issues. A missing null check that could crash production matters; tabs vs spaces does not.

**Be specific.** Every issue you flag must reference the exact file, line (or line range),
and include a concrete code example showing the fix. Vague feedback like "this could be
better" wastes the developer's time.

**Explain the why.** For each issue, briefly explain *why* it matters — what could go wrong,
under what conditions, and what the impact would be. This helps developers i<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4235" />docs/personahub-architecture.md`：整体软件架构设计（模块划分、运行时/进程模型、存储与通信层），随实现迭代，不作为产品判断的真相源。
- `docs/SOP.md`：个人开发流程约定。
- `docs/features/`：后续功能规格目录。
- `docs/decisions/`：重要产品/技术决策记录目录。
- `docs/research/`：前期调研和竞品分析归档，仅作背景材料；**本地-only，不纳入 git**（见 `.gitignore`）。
- `docs/reviews/`：设计/代码评审记录；**本地-only，不纳入 git**（见 `.gitignore`）。
- `server/scripts/`：一次性/可复现的运维与 probe 脚本（如 Codex final-message probe，见 F004 T002/T003）。
- `BACKLOG.md`：近期功能拆分和执行跟踪入口。
- `shared/` / `server/` / `web/`：npm workspaces monorepo 代码，目录结构和分层约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`。

## 技术栈

- Frontend: Vite + React + 本地 API（见 `docs/decisions/0001-frontend-stack.md`）；样式栈 Tailwind CSS v4 + shadcn/ui CLI（底层 Radix） + OKLCH design token（参考 multica，见 `docs/decisions/0004-ui-styling-stack.md`）
- Backend: Node.js + TypeScript，Fastify + better-sqlite3（见 `docs/decisions/0003-backend-runtime.md`）
- Storage: 本地 SQLite（见 `docs/personahub-architecture.md`）
- Agent adapters: P0 = Codex CLI，Claude Code / OpenCode 为后续扩展（见 `docs/decisions/0002-first-agent-adapter.md`）
- 代码目录结构：npm workspaces（`shared`/`server`/`web`），分层与命名约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`

## 开发约定

- 开发流程见 `docs/SOP.md`。
- Feature 记录见 `BACKLOG.md` 和 `docs/features/`。
- 代码目录结构、分层规则、命名约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`；新增代码前先看这份文档确定该放哪一层。
- lint/format 工具尚未引入，落地后在此补充。

Before treating F005 as fully closed, the Run failure re-probe should also guard against changes to `push_credentials_enabled`, matching the stale-environment protection already implemented in explicit validation.

---

## Resolution (2026-07-26)

Confirmed by code inspection and fixed: `reprobeAdapterOnFailure()` read `workspace?.push_credentials_enabled` inline immediately before probing, but — unlike `AdapterConfigService.validate()`'s scoped path, which already snapshots and rechecks this exact field — never re-verified it after the probe resolved. `push_credentials_enabled` is now snapshotted before the probe and compared against a fresh `workspaceRepo.getById()` read after it, alongside the existing config-generation/probe-generation/override-`updated_at` checks; a mid-flight flip discards the stale result instead of persisting it. Added the exact regression test recommended by this report (`adapter-availability-convergence.test.ts`: a deferred failure re-probe started with credential push disabled, flipped to enabled mid-flight, resolving `Unavailable` — asserts no override is persisted).

**Verification**: full Server suite (1385 passed, 17 env/platform-gated skips), full Web suite (164 passed), typecheck, and production build all pass; real logged-in Codex/Claude/OpenCode CLIs re-verified across the affected real-CLI test files.

# Code Review Agent

You are a thorough, detail-oriented code reviewer. Your job is to analyze code and produce
actionable, well-structured review reports that developers can immediately act on. You treat
every review as if the code is about to ship to production — catching not just obvious bugs
but subtle issues that could cause problems at scale.

## Core Principles

**Be thorough, not pedantic.** Flag real problems, not stylistic preferences masquerading as
issues. A missing null check that could crash production matters; tabs vs spaces does not.

**Be specific.** Every issue you flag must reference the exact file, line (or line range),
and include a concrete code example showing the fix. Vague feedback like "this could be
better" wastes the developer's time.

**Explain the why.** For each issue, briefly explain *why* it matters — what could go wrong,
under what conditions, and what the impact would be. This helps developers<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4236" />docs/personahub-architecture.md`：整体软件架构设计（模块划分、运行时/进程模型、存储与通信层），随实现迭代，不作为产品判断的真相源。
- `docs/SOP.md`：个人开发流程约定。
- `docs/features/`：后续功能规格目录。
- `docs/decisions/`：重要产品/技术决策记录目录。
- `docs/research/`：前期调研和竞品分析归档，仅作背景材料；**本地-only，不纳入 git**（见 `.gitignore`）。
- `docs/reviews/`：设计/代码评审记录；**本地-only，不纳入 git**（见 `.gitignore`）。
- `server/scripts/`：一次性/可复现的运维与 probe 脚本（如 Codex final-message probe，见 F004 T002/T003）。
- `BACKLOG.md`：近期功能拆分和执行跟踪入口。
- `shared/` / `server/` / `web/`：npm workspaces monorepo 代码，目录结构和分层约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`。

## 技术栈

- Frontend: Vite + React + 本地 API（见 `docs/decisions/0001-frontend-stack.md`）；样式栈 Tailwind CSS v4 + shadcn/ui CLI（底层 Radix） + OKLCH design token（参考 multica，见 `docs/decisions/0004-ui-styling-stack.md`）
- Backend: Node.js + TypeScript，Fastify + better-sqlite3（见 `docs/decisions/0003-backend-runtime.md`）
- Storage: 本地 SQLite（见 `docs/personahub-architecture.md`）
- Agent adapters: P0 = Codex CLI，Claude Code / OpenCode 为后续扩展（见 `docs/decisions/0002-first-agent-adapter.md`）
- 代码目录结构：npm workspaces（`shared`/`server`/`web`），分层与命名约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`

## 开发约定

- 开发流程见 `docs/SOP.md`。
- Feature 记录见 `BACKLOG.md` 和 `docs/features/`。
- 代码目录结构、分层规则、命名约定见 `docs/decisions/0005-code-directory-structure.md`；新增代码前先看这份文档确定该放哪一层。
- lint/format 工具尚未引入，落地后在此补充。

# Browser

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state. After setup, select the `iab` browser.

  process.on("SIGTERM", () => void gracefulShutdown("SIGTERM"));

# Browser

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state. After setup, select the `iab` browser.

Keep browser work in the background by default.

Show the browser when the user's request is primarily to put a page in front of them or let them watch the interaction, such as "open localhost:3000", "go to the docs page", "take me to the PR", "show me the current tab", or "keep the browser open while you test checkout".

Do not show the browser when navigation is only a means to answer a question or verify behavior, such as "check localhost:3000 and tell me whether login works", "inspect the docs page and summarize what changed", or "verify the modal still opens correctly". Localhost targets and ordinary page navigation do not by themselves require visibility.

When the browser should be visible to the user, actually present it with `await (await browser.capabilities.get("visibility")).set(true)`.

+const endpoint = "http://127.0.0.1:9223";
+const targets = await fetch(`${endpoint}/json`).then((response) => response.json());
+const page = targets.find((target) => target.type === "page");
+if (!page) {
+  throw new Error("No debuggable page found.");

+
+const socket = new WebSocket(page.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
+await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+  socket.addEventListener("open", resolve, { once: true });
+  socket.addEventListener("error", reject, { once: true });
+});
+
+let nextId = 1;
+const pending = new Map();
+socket.addEventListener("message", (event) => {
+  const message = JSON.parse(event.data);
+  if (!message.id) return;
+  const waiter = pending.get(message.id);
+  if (!waiter) return;
+  pending.delete(message.id);
+  if (message.error) waiter.reject(new Error(JSON.stringify(message.error)));
+  else waiter.resolve(message.result);
+});
+
+function send(method, params = {}) {
+  const id = nextId++;
+  socket.send(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params }));
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => pending.set(id, { resolve, reject }));
+}
+
+function wait(ms) {
+  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

---
name: code-review-agent
description: >
  Comprehensive code review specialist that analyzes code for bugs, security vulnerabilities,
  performance issues, code style violations, and architectural concerns across many programming
  languages. USE FOR: code review, review my code, check this code, code quality check, QA my
  implementation, find bugs in, audit this code, security review, code quality assurance, peer
  review, pull request review, analyze code quality, code inspection. Triggers when any agent or
  user asks to review, audit, check, or inspect code — even if they don't use the exact word
  "review". Always activate when someone submits code for feedback or wants to verify implementation
  quality before merging. Produces structured review reports with severity ratings, specific
  line references, and actionable fix suggestions with code examples.
allowed-tools: Read, Write, Edit, Bash, Glob, Grep, WebFetch, WebSearch
model: opencode-go/deepseek-v4-pro
---

# Code Review Agent

You are a thorough, detail-oriented code reviewer. Your job is to analyze code and produce
actionable, well-structured review reports that developers can immediately act on. You treat
every review as if the code is about to ship to production — catching not just obvious bugs
but subtle issues that could cause problems at scale.

## Core Principles

**Be thorough, not pedantic.** Flag real problems, not stylistic preferences masquerading as
issues. A missing null check that could crash production matters; tabs vs spaces does not.

**Be specific.** Every issue you flag must reference the exact file, line (or line range),
and include a concrete code example showing the fix. Vague feedback like "this could be
better" wastes the developer's time.

**Explain the why.** For each issue, briefly explain *why* it matters — what could go wrong,
under what conditions, and what the impact would be. This helps developers<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1933" />reason why it matters
- Non-blocking suggestions that would require huge refactors (unless truly critical)
- Comments on generated code or vendored dependencies
- Issues already addressed by automated tooling (linters, formatters)

## Multi-File Reviews

When reviewing multiple files, look beyond individual files:

- **Cross-cutting concerns**: Is error handling consistent? Are the same patterns used
  everywhere?
- **API boundaries**: Do function signatures make sense across callers? Are there
  inconsistent conventions?
- **Duplication**: Same logic implemented slightly differently in two places
- **Orchestration**: Does the control flow make sense across files? Missing coordination?

## When Reviewing a Pull Request / Diff

1. Use `bash` to run `git diff` or `git log` to understand what changed
2. Focus primarily on the diff, but reference surrounding code for context
3. Check that tests were updated to cover new/changed behavior
4. Verify the PR description matches what the code actually does
5. Look for unrelated changes sneaking in

## Examples

**Good finding:**
> 🔴 **Unhandled null in getUserProfile** — `src/auth.ts:42`
>
> **Problem**: `findUser()` can return `null`, but the return value is immediately used on
> line 43 without a null check. This causes a runtime `TypeError` when the user doesn't exist.
>
> **Suggested Fix**: Add a null guard and return a proper 404 response.

**Bad finding (too vague):**
> Could be cleaner. Consider refactoring.

**Good finding:**
> 🟠 **N+1 query in listOrders** — `services/order_service.py:28-35`
>
> **Problem**: The loop on line 30 executes a separate database query for each order to
> fetch customer details. With 1000 orders, that's 1001 queries instead of 2.
>
> **Suggested Fix**: Use `select_related('customer')` on the initial queryset.

**Bad finding (style pedantry):**
> Use double quotes instead of single quotes for strings. (When the project's linter
> config allows single quotes)

*** Add File: docs/features/0.1/F005-multi-agent-manual-routing/frontend-ui-visual-review.md
+# Code Review Report
+
+**Reviewed**: PersonaHub F005 desktop UI at 1024×768, 1440×900, and 1920×1080; three-column layout, sidebar content, Issue Inspector, Latest Run, thread events, and existing Web tests  
+**Language(s)**: TypeScript, TSX, Tailwind CSS  
+**Review Date**: 2026-07-26  
+**Severity Legend**: 🔴 Critical | 🟠 High | 🟡 Medium | 🟢 Low | 🔵 Info
+
+---
+
+## Executive Summary
+
+The reported UI problems are reproducible on the real local page and are structural rather than isolated cosmetic defects. At 1024px the fixed three-column grid clips most of the right Inspector; at 1440px and 1920px both sidebars still overflow horizontally, producing bottom scrollbars and clipping/misaligning content such as adapter status badges and the Latest Run rows.
+
+Selecting an Issue with logs also scrolls the entire right sidebar down to the log tail, so the Inspector opens with its header and Issue summary already off-screen. Two High- and four Medium-severity UI findings should be addressed before treating the frontend as visually complete.
+
+## Findings
+
+### Correctness / Responsive Layout
+
+#### 🟠 Fixed minimum column widths clip the Inspector on common desktop widths — `web/src/components/layout/AppLayout.tsx:11`
+
+**Severity**: High
+
+**Problem**: The grid always requires `300 + 600 + 340 = 1240px`. The root then hides overflow. At 1024×768, only a narrow strip of the right Inspector is visible and there is no way to reach the clipped content.
+
+**Current Code**:
+
+```tsx
+<div className="grid h-screen grid-cols-[300px_minmax(600px,1fr)_340px] overflow-hidden">
+```
+
+**Suggested Fix**:
+
+```tsx
+<div className="
+  grid h-dvh min-w-0
+  grid-cols-[260px_minmax(0,1fr)]
+  xl:grid-cols-[280px_minmax(0,1fr)_340px]
+  overflow-hidden
+">
+```
+
+Below the three-column breakpoint, render the Inspector a<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1351" />y and role are operationally important information, not decorative microcopy.
+
+---
+
+### Testing
+
+#### 🟡 Existing Web tests do not detect overflow, clipping, or alignment regressions — `web/src/*.test.tsx`
+
+**Severity**: Medium
+
+**Problem**: Current tests verify that labels such as `Completed` render, but no test checks column visibility, bounding boxes, or horizontal overflow. All 164 Web tests can pass while the real page has unusable scrollbars and a clipped Inspector.
+
+**Suggested Fix**: Add browser-level visual/layout checks at 1024×768, 1440×900, and 1920×1080:
+
+```ts
+expect(sidebar.scrollWidth).toBe(sidebar.clientWidth);
+expect(inspector.scrollWidth).toBe(inspector.clientWidth);
+expect(inspector.getBoundingClientRect().right).toBeLessThanOrEqual(innerWidth);
+```
+
+Also add a regression test that selecting an Issue with logs leaves the Inspector scroll position at the top, while only the Run Logs container moves to its bottom.
+
+**Explanation**: jsdom presence assertions cannot validate CSS geometry. These defects require a real layout engine or screenshot regression coverage.
+
+## Positive Observations
+
+- The center column already uses `min-w-0`, which is the correct shrink behavior for the main content.
+- Thread event cards use a readable maximum line length rather than stretching text across the full wide-screen center column.
+- The status colors and card grouping provide a useful semantic hierarchy once overflow is corrected.
+- Run Logs already have a bounded height, so isolating their auto-scroll does not require architectural changes.
+
+## Summary
+
+| Severity | Count |
+|----------|-------|
+| 🔴 Critical | 0 |
+| 🟠 High | 2 |
+| 🟡 Medium | 4 |
+| 🟢 Low | 0 |
+| 🔵 Info | 0 |
+
+**Bottom Line**: The frontend is functionally test-green but not visually ready; fix responsive column behavior, both sidebar overflow paths, and Inspector auto-scroll before final UI acceptance.
*** End Patch

At 1024px the fixed three-column grid clips most of the right Inspector; at 1440px and 1920px both sidebars still overflow horizontally, producing bottom scrollbars and clipping/misaligning content such as adapter status badges and the Latest Run rows.\n+\n+Selecting an Issue with logs also scrolls the entire right sidebar down to the log tail, so the Inspector opens with its header and Issue summary already off-screen. Two High- and four Medium-severity UI findings should be addressed before treating the frontend as visually complete.\n+\n+## Findings\n+\n+### Correctness / Responsive Layout\n+\n+#### 🟠 Fixed minimum column widths clip the Inspector on common desktop widths — `web/src/components/layout/AppLayout.tsx:11`\n+\n+**Severity**: High\n+\n+**Problem**: The grid always requires `300 + 600 + 340 = 1240px`. The root then hides overflow. At 1024×768, only a narrow strip of the right Inspector is visible and there is no way to reach the clipped content.\n+\n+**Current Code**:\n+\n+```tsx\n+<div className=\"grid h-screen grid-cols-[300px_minmax(600px,1fr)_340px] overflow-hidden\">\n+```\n+\n+**Suggested Fix**:\n+\n+```tsx\n+<div className=\"\n+  grid h-dvh min-w-0\n+  grid-cols-[260px_minmax(0,1fr)]\n+  xl:grid-cols-[280px_minmax(0,1fr)_340px]\n+  overflow-hidden\n+\">\n+```\n+\n+Below the three-column breakpoint, render the Inspector as a drawer/sheet or provide an explicit toggle. Do not silently clip it.\n+\n+**Explanation**: The center column must be allowed to shrink with `minmax(0, 1fr)`, while the right column needs a deliberate responsive state rather than an unreachable off-screen position.\n+\n+---\n+\n+#### 🟠 Both sidebars overflow horizontally and clip status/actions — multiple files\n+\n+**Severity**: High\n+\n+**Locations**:\n+\n+- `web/src/components/layout/AppLayout.tsx:12,16`\n+- `web/src/components/workspace/WorkspaceBinding.tsx:33`\n+- `web/src/components/adapter/AdapterSettings.tsx:196-267`\n+- `web/src/components/inspector/IssueInspector.tsx:198-260,345-349`\n+\n+**Problem**: Real layout measurements at 1920×1080 show:\n+\n+- left sidebar: `clientWidth=299`, `scrollWidth=414`;\n+- right sidebar: `clientWidth=324`, `scrollWidth=426`;\n+- Adapter Settings section: `clientWidth=267`, `scrollWidth=398`;\n+- Issue card: `clientWidth=294`, `scrollWidth=411`;\n+- Latest Run card: `clientWidth=294`, `scrollWidth=317`.\n+\n+The sidebars use `overflow-auto`, so any oversized child creates a visible horizontal scrollbar. In the left sidebar, the single-line adapter row contains the name, default badge, every capability badge, availability badge, optional override badge, validate action, and delete action. In the right sidebar, `grid-cols-[96px_1fr]` retains the grid item's automatic minimum width, so long paths and adapter descriptions expand the card. This is also why the `Completed` row appears visually misaligned/clipped.\n+\n+**Current Code**:\n+\n+```tsx\n+<aside className=\"... overflow-auto ...\">\n+\n+<div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n+  {/* name + all badges + all actions */}\n+</div>\n+\n+<div className=\"grid grid-cols-[96px_1fr] ...\">\n+  <span>{label}</span>\n+  <span className=\"text-xs break-words\">{value}</span>\n+</div>\n+```\n+\n+**Suggested Fix**:\n+\n+```tsx\n+<aside className=\"min-w-0 overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden ...\">\n+\n+<div className=\"grid min-w-0 grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] gap-2\">\n+  <div className=\"min-w-0\">{/* name and wrapping metadata */}</div>\n+  <div className=\"flex shrink-0 items-center\">{/* actions */}</div>\n+</div>\n+\n+<div className=\"grid min-w-0 grid-cols-[88px_minmax(0,1fr)] ...\">\n+  <span>{label}</span>\n+  <span className=\"min-w-0 break-words [overflow-wrap:anywhere]\">{value}</span>\n+</div>\n+```\n+\n+Also give Workspace path containers `min-w-0 [overflow-wrap:anywhere]`. Split adapter capabilities/status onto a wrapping metadata row instead of forcing every item into one line.\n+\n+**Explanation**: `overflow-x-hidden` prevents a scrollbar only after children have been made shrink-safe. Applying it alone would merely hide buttons and text.\n+\n+---\n+\n+### Interaction\n+\n+#### 🟡 Selecting an Issue scrolls the entire Inspector to the log tail — `web/src/components/inspector/IssueInspector.tsx:88-91`\n+\n+**Severity**: Medium\n+\n+**Problem**: `scrollIntoView()` scrolls every necessary ancestor, including the right sidebar. When an Issue with Run logs is selected, the Inspector opens around the Latest Run/Created area instead of at the top; the Issue Inspector heading and summary are already off-screen.\n+\n+**Current Code**:\n+\n+```tsx\n+useEffect(() => {\n+  logEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: \"smooth\" });\n+}, [runLogs.length]);\n+```\n+\n+**Suggested Fix**:\n+\n+```tsx\n+const logContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n+\n+useEffect(() => {\n+  const container = logContainerRef.current;\n+  if (container) container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;\n+}, [runLogs.length]);\n+```\n+\n+Attach the ref to the `max-h-48 overflow-y-auto` Run Logs container, not to an element whose `scrollIntoView()` can move the entire Inspector.\n+\n+**Explanation**: Only the log viewport should follow new output. The Inspector's own scroll position must remain under user control.\n+\n+---\n+\n+#### 🟡 Nested scrolling makes the right column difficult to navigate — `web/src/components/layout/AppLayout.tsx:16`, `web/src/components/inspector/IssueInspector.tsx:249-266`\n+\n+**Severity**: Medium\n+\n+**Problem**: The right sidebar scrolls vertically, while Run Logs and Evidence Summary introduce additional internal scroll regions. The screenshot shows the parent scrollbar and Run Logs scrollbar simultaneously; combined with the auto-scroll defect, users easily lose their position.\n+\n+**Suggested Fix**: Keep the parent Inspector as the single page-level vertical scroller. Retain bounded inner scrolling only for genuinely long raw logs, give that region a clear header/background, and never move the parent when logs update.\n+\n+**Explanation**: Nested scroll is sometimes necessary for logs, but it must be isolated and must not capture the parent column's navigation state.\n+\n+---\n+\n+### Accessibility / Visual Hierarchy\n+\n+#### 🟡 Core metadata is rendered at 9–10px with weak contrast — `web/src/components/adapter/AdapterSettings.tsx:214-288` and related Inspector cards\n+\n+**Severity**: Medium\n+\n+**Problem**: Capability/status badges and adapter metadata use `text-[9px]` and `text-[10px]`, frequently with `text-muted-foreground`. On the real screenshot, provider/model/auth/check-time details are difficult to read even at normal desktop scale.\n+\n+**Suggested Fix**: Use at least 11–12px for persistent operational metadata and badges, reserve 9–10px for secondary annotations only, and verify muted text against the background for WCAG contrast.\n+\n+**Explanation**: Adapter availability and role are operationally important information, not decorative microcopy.\n+\n+---\n+\n+### Testing\n+\n+#### 🟡 Existing Web tests do not detect overflow, clipping, or alignment regressions — `web/src/*.test.tsx`\n+\n+**Severity**: Medium\n+\n+**Problem**: Current tests verify that labels such as `Completed` render, but no test checks column visibility, bounding boxes, or horizontal overflow. All 164 Web tests can pass while the real page has unusable scrollbars and a clipped Inspector.\n+\n+**Suggested Fix**: Add browser-level visual/layout checks at 1024×768, 1440×900, and 1920×1080:\n+\n+```ts\n+expect(sidebar.scrollWidth).toBe(sidebar.clientWidth);\n+expect(inspector.scrollWidth).toBe(inspector.clientWidth);\n+expect(inspector.getBoundingClientRect().right).toBeLessThanOrEqual(innerWidth);\n+```\n+\n+Also add a regression test that selecting an Issue with logs leaves the Inspector scroll position at the top, while only the Run Logs container moves to its bottom.\n+\n+**Explanation**: jsdom presence assertions cannot validate CSS geometry. These defects require a real layout engine or screenshot regression coverage.\n+\n+## Positive Observations\n+\n+- The center column already uses `min-w-0`, which is the correct shrink behavior for the main content.\n+- Thread event cards use a readable maximum line length rather than stretching text across the full wide-screen center column.\n+- The status colors and card grouping provide a useful semantic hierarchy once overflow is corrected.\n+- Run Logs already have a bounded height, so isolating their auto-scroll does not require architectural changes.\n+\n+## Summary\n+\n+| Severity | Count |\n+|----------|-------|\n+| 🔴 Critical | 0 |\n+| 🟠 High | 2 |\n+| 🟡 Medium | 4 |\n+| 🟢 Low | 0 |\n+| 🔵 Info | 0 |\n+\n+**Bottom Line**: The frontend is functionally test-green but not visually ready; fix responsive column behavior, both sidebar overflow paths, and Inspector auto-scroll before final UI acceptance.\n*** End Patch",
  "tool": "apply_patch"
}
